<tr><td>↳近 foul</td><td>/ˈfaʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>玷污（名誉）；弄脏</td><td>foul one's good name<br>玷污自己的好名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal fouled the good name he had spent decades building.  这桩丑闻玷污了他数十年积累的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fusty</td><td>/ˈfʌsti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有霉味的，霉臭的；过时的</td><td>a fusty old room<br>一间霉味的旧房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They opened every window to air out the fusty old room.  他们打开每扇窗给这间霉味的旧房间通风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malodorous</td><td>/ˌmælˈoʊdərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的</td><td>malodorous waste<br>恶臭的废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors traced the malodorous discharge to a nearby factory.  检查人员追查到那股恶臭排放来自附近一家工厂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noisome</td><td>/ˈnɔɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的；令人极厌恶的</td><td>a noisome odor<br>一股恶臭气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers refused to enter the noisome tunnel without masks.  工人们不戴口罩不肯进入那条恶臭的隧道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reeking</td><td>/ˈriːkɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的，散发臭味的</td><td>reeking of smoke<br>散发着烟味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came home reeking of fish and diesel oil.  他回家时浑身鱼味和柴油味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smelly</td><td>/ˈsmeli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难闻的，有臭味的</td><td>smelly socks<br>有臭味的袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bin in the corner was getting distinctly smelly.  角落里的垃圾桶明显开始发臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stinky</td><td>/ˈstɪŋki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>臭的，恶臭的</td><td>a stinky cheese<br>一种臭味的奶酪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That stinky cheese tastes far better than it smells.  那种臭奶酪的味道远比气味好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ambrosial</td><td>/æmˈbroʊziəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特别美味的；芬芳的</td><td>an ambrosial dessert<br>美味无比的甜点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup had an ambrosial aroma that filled the whole house.  这汤散发的美妙香气弥漫了整座房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aromatic</td><td>/ˌerəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>芳香的</td><td>aromatic herbs<br>芳香的草药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen was full of aromatic spices from the market.  厨房里满是从市场买来的芳香香料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balmy</td><td>/ˈbɑːmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和宜人的</td><td>a balmy evening<br>温暖宜人的傍晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We sat outside enjoying the balmy air of early summer.  我们坐在外面享受初夏温和宜人的空气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fragrant</td><td>/ˈfreɪɡrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>芳香的，香的</td><td>fragrant flowers<br>芳香的花朵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen was fragrant with fresh bread and cinnamon.  厨房里弥漫着新鲜面包和肉桂的香气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 redolent</td><td>/ˈredələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>芳香的；令人想起…的</td><td>redolent of spices<br>散发香料气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old room was redolent of pipe smoke and leather.  那间旧屋弥漫着烟斗烟味和皮革气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scented</td><td>/ˈsentɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有香味的，加香的</td><td>scented candles<br>香味蜡烛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wrote all her letters on pale scented paper.  她所有的信都写在淡色的香味纸上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fetter</b></td><td>/ˈfetər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚</td><td>the fetters of tradition<br>传统的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy family obligations fettered her ambition for many long years.  沉重的家庭责任多年来束缚了她的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chain</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>连接，用链系住；链条</td><td>chain the parts together<br>把各部分连起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chained his bicycle to the railing outside the shop.  他把自行车锁在店外的栏杆上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clog</td><td>/klɑːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>阻碍物</td><td>a clog on progress<br>进步的障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old regulations act as a clog on economic progress.  陈旧的法规成了经济进步的阻碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enfetter</del></td><td>/ɛnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚，使受奴役</td><td>enfetter the human spirit<br>束缚人的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old superstitions still enfetter the minds of many villagers.  旧迷信仍束缚着许多村民的思想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 manacle</del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrain</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，约束，制止</td><td>restrain his temper<br>克制他的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could hardly restrain her laughter during the speech.  演讲时她几乎忍不住笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enfranchise</td><td>/ɪnˈfræntʃaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予…权利（例如选举权）；解放</td><td>enfranchise women<br>赋予妇女选举权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amendment enfranchised millions of citizens for the first time.  该修正案首次赋予数百万公民选举权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 free</td><td>/ˈfriː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，解放；自由的</td><td>free the hostages<br>释放人质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Negotiators finally persuaded the gunmen to free the hostages.  谈判者最终说服枪手释放了人质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbind</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，松开，释放</td><td>unbind the prisoner<br>解开囚犯的绑缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She knelt down to unbind the wounded man's hands.  她跪下来解开那名伤者被绑的双手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unfetter</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解除束缚，使自由</td><td>unfetter the market<br>解除对市场的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform unfettered small firms and let them compete freely.  改革解除了对小企业的束缚，让它们自由竞争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unshackle</td><td>/ʌnˈʃækəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开束缚，释放</td><td>unshackle the economy<br>解除对经济的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reforms unshackled small businesses from needless paperwork.  改革让小企业摆脱了无谓的文书负担。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fiasco</b></td><td>/fiˈæskoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大失败</td><td>end in a fiasco<br>以惨败告终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poorly planned launch party ended in a complete fiasco.  筹划糟糕的发布会以彻底的失败告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catastrophe</td><td>/kəˈtæstrəfi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；彻底的失败</td><td>an ecological catastrophe<br>生态大灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The oil spill became an ecological catastrophe for the whole coast.  漏油事件成为整个海岸的生态大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debacle</td><td>/deɪˈbɑːkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>溃败</td><td>an economic debacle<br>经济上的溃败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign ended in a debacle that cost the party three seats.  这场竞选以溃败收场，使该党丢掉三个席位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disaster</td><td>/dɪˈzæstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灾难；彻底的失败</td><td>a natural disaster<br>自然灾害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opening night was a disaster from the very first scene.  首演之夜从第一场戏起就是一场彻底的失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 failure</td><td>/ˈfeɪljər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失败，失败的事物</td><td>a complete failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The launch was a costly failure watched by millions.  这次发射是数百万人围观的昂贵失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blockbuster</td><td>/ˈblɑːkbʌstər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>非常成功的作品，大片</td><td>a summer blockbuster<br>暑期大片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The studio needed a blockbuster to cover last year's losses.  制片厂需要一部大片来弥补去年的亏损。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 success</td><td>/səkˈses/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>成功；成功的人或事</td><td>a resounding success<br>巨大的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fundraising campaign was a resounding success beyond all forecasts.  这次筹款活动大获成功，远超所有预测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fickle</b></td><td>/ˈfɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的</td><td>fickle public taste<br>易变的大众品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public taste is fickle, so last year's hit sells poorly now.  大众品味易变，去年的热门如今销量很差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capricious</td><td>/kəˈprɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，善变的</td><td>capricious weather<br>变化无常的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The capricious wind kept shifting during the whole race.  整场比赛中变化无常的风一直在转向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mercurial</td><td>/mɜːrˈkjʊriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（情绪）善变的</td><td>a mercurial temper<br>善变的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mercurial temper made colleagues hesitate before bringing him any bad news.  他善变的脾气让同事在向他报告坏消息前都要犹豫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutable</td><td>/ˈmjuːtəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，可变的</td><td>mutable rules<br>可变的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fashion is mutable, but good tailoring never goes away.  时尚易变，但好裁剪永不过时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperamental</td><td>/ˌtemprəˈmentəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜怒无常的；不稳定的</td><td>a temperamental artist<br>喜怒无常的艺术家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old printer is temperamental on humid summer days.  这台旧打印机在潮湿的夏日很不稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volatile</td><td>/ˈvɑːlətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多变的</td><td>a volatile stock market<br>多变的股市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors hesitated because the stock market stayed volatile all spring.  由于股市整个春天都动荡多变，投资者犹豫不决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不变的，恒定的；忠诚的</td><td>a constant friend<br>忠诚不变的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her support remained constant through every difficult year.  她的支持在每个艰难年头都不曾改变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immutable</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可变的</td><td>an immutable law of nature<br>不可变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gravity is an immutable law that no clever argument can suspend.  引力是不可变的法则，任何巧辩都无法使它失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invariable</td><td>/ɪnˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不变的，恒定的</td><td>an invariable routine<br>一成不变的常规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His morning routine has been invariable for twenty years.  他的晨间习惯二十年来一成不变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 steady</td><td>/ˈstedi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稳定的，持续不变的</td><td>a steady stream of visitors<br>络绎不绝的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales have shown steady growth for eight straight quarters.  销售额已连续八个季度稳定增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fictitious</b></td><td>/fɪkˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚构的</td><td>a fictitious name<br>虚构的名字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He checked into the hotel under a fictitious name.  他用一个虚构的名字入住了旅馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chimerical</td><td>/kɪˈmerəkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空想的，荒诞不经的</td><td>a chimerical plan<br>空想的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their plan to colonize Mars by 2030 seems chimerical.  他们2030年殖民火星的计划显得荒诞不经。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabricated</td><td>/ˈfæbrɪˌkeɪtəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>编造的，伪造的</td><td>fabricated evidence<br>伪造的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness admitted that his entire story was fabricated.  证人承认他的整个说法都是编造的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabulous</td><td>/ˈfæbjələs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幻想中的，不真实的；极好的</td><td>fabulous creatures<br>神话中的生物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps often showed fabulous creatures swimming in distant seas.  古地图上常画着在远海游动的神话生物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fictional</td><td>/ˈfɪkʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚构的，小说中的</td><td>a fictional character<br>虚构的人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sherlock Holmes is a fictional character loved by millions.  夏洛克·福尔摩斯是深受数百万人喜爱的虚构人物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imagined</td><td>/ɪˈmædʒɪnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>想象出的，臆想的</td><td>an imagined threat<br>臆想的威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fears were imagined rather than based on real danger.  她的恐惧是臆想的，而非基于真实危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invented</td><td>/ɪnˈventɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>编造的，虚构的</td><td>an invented story<br>编造的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reporter used an invented quote to spice up the article.  记者用编造的引语给文章增色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 actual</td><td>/ˈæktʃuəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，实际的</td><td>the actual cost<br>实际成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actual cost was far higher than the original estimate.  实际成本远高于最初的估价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 existing</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>现有的，现存的</td><td>existing regulations<br>现有的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We can upgrade the existing system instead of buying a new one.  我们可以升级现有系统，而不必新买一套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 real</td><td>/ˈriːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a real event<br>真实事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a real event from 1943.  这部电影基于1943年的一件真实事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fidelity</b></td><td>/fɪˈdeləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚</td><td>fidelity to his promise<br>对诺言的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fidelity to the promise surprised everyone who knew him.  他对诺言的忠诚让所有认识他的人都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adherence</td><td>/ədˈhɪrəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵守，坚持；忠诚</td><td>strict adherence to rules<br>严格遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict adherence to the safety rules prevented any accidents.  严格遵守安全规程避免了任何事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allegiance</td><td>/əˈliːdʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，效忠</td><td>swear allegiance to<br>宣誓效忠于</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New citizens pledge allegiance to the country at the ceremony.  新公民在仪式上宣誓效忠国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commitment</td><td>/kəˈmɪtmənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>致力，投入；承诺</td><td>a firm commitment to<br>对…的坚定承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed a deep commitment to protecting local wildlife.  她表现出保护当地野生动物的坚定投入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devotion</td><td>/dɪˈvoʊʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，挚爱；献身</td><td>devotion to duty<br>忠于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her devotion to her students earned her wide respect.  她对学生的挚爱赢得了广泛的尊敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faith</td><td>/ˈfeɪθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>信任，信念；信仰</td><td>have faith in<br>信任，相信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept faith in their coach through a terrible season.  在糟糕的赛季里他们始终信任教练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loyalty</td><td>/ˈlɔɪəlti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，忠实</td><td>loyalty to the team<br>对团队的忠诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of loyalty finally earned him the manager's trust.  多年的忠诚终于赢得了经理的信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 piety</td><td>/ˈpaɪəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>虔诚；孝顺</td><td>filial piety<br>孝道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman was admired for her quiet piety and charity.  这位老妇人因其内敛的虔诚与善行而受人敬重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disloyalty</td><td>/dɪsˈlɔɪəlti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>accused of disloyalty<br>被指不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of disloyalty to the party leadership.  他被指对党的领导层不忠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infidelity</td><td>/ˌɪnfɪˈdelɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>marital infidelity<br>婚内不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple separated after years of quarrels and infidelity.  这对夫妻在多年争吵和背叛后分居了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>figurative</b></td><td>/ˈfɪɡərətɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>比喻的</td><td>figurative language<br>比喻性的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poets rely on figurative language to suggest what plain words cannot.  诗人依靠比喻性的语言表达平实词句无法传达的东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extended</td><td>/ɪkˈstendəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引申的；延长的</td><td>an extended meaning<br>引申义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The word carries an extended meaning in classical poetry.  这个词在古典诗歌中有引申义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 metaphorical</td><td>/ˌmetəˈfɔːrɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐喻的，比喻的</td><td>a metaphorical expression<br>比喻性的表达</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was full of metaphorical expressions about journeys.  他的演讲充满关于旅程的比喻表达。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 literal</td><td>/ˈlɪtərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>字面的，本义的</td><td>the literal meaning<br>字面意思</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Take the literal meaning first, then look for irony.  先取字面意思，再寻找讽喻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fractious</b></td><td>/ˈfrækʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以管束的</td><td>a fractious child<br>难管束的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fractious child refused to sit still during the whole flight.  这个难以管束的孩子整趟飞行都不肯安坐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perverse</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反常固执的，任性的</td><td>take perverse pleasure<br>反常地感到快意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He takes perverse pleasure in arguing against every popular opinion.  他反常地乐于反驳每一个流行观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untoward</td><td>/ʌnˈtɔːrd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的；难以驯服的，难以驾驭的</td><td>an untoward incident<br>意外的不幸事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing untoward happened during the long flight across the ocean.  在这趟跨洋长途飞行中没有发生任何不幸的意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wayward</td><td>/ˈweɪwərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，不服管束的</td><td>a wayward son<br>不服管束的儿子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their wayward son ignored every rule his patient parents set.  他们不服管束的儿子无视父母耐心定下的每条规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 subdued</td><td>/səbˈduːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的；柔和低沉的</td><td>in a subdued voice<br>用压低的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd grew subdued once the announcement was finally made.  消息宣布后，人群变得安静顺从。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>filibuster</b></td><td>/ˈfɪlɪbʌstər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>（议会中）阻挠议事</td><td>launch a filibuster<br>发起议事阻挠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator launched a filibuster to delay the final vote.  那位参议员发起阻挠议事来拖延最终表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delay</td><td>/dɪˈleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>延迟，推迟</td><td>delay the departure<br>推迟出发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fog delayed the flight by more than four hours.  大雾使航班延误了四个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hindrance</td><td>/ˈhɪndrəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>妨碍，障碍</td><td>a hindrance to progress<br>进步的障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor roads are a serious hindrance to trade here.  糟糕的道路是这里贸易的严重障碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impediment</td><td>/ɪmˈpedɪmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>妨碍物，障碍</td><td>an impediment to trade<br>贸易的障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor roads are a serious impediment to rural development.  路况差是农村发展的严重障碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstruction</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>障碍物；阻挠</td><td>an obstruction in the road<br>路上的障碍物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police removed the obstruction that had blocked traffic for hours.  警方清除了堵塞交通数小时的障碍物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 postponement</td><td>/poʊstˈpoʊnmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>延期，推迟</td><td>postponement of the trial<br>审判延期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The postponement of the trial angered the victim's family.  审判延期激怒了受害者的家人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 procrastination</td><td>/prəˌkræstəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>拖延，耽误</td><td>endless procrastination<br>无休止的拖延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of procrastination left the report unfinished.  数月的拖延使报告仍未完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>filter</b></td><td>/ˈfɪltər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>过滤</td><td>filter out impurities<br>滤除杂质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This device filters out impurities before the water reaches the tank.  这个装置在水进入水箱前滤除杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 screen</td><td>/skriːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>掩护物，屏障；掩护，遮蔽</td><td>a screen of trees<br>一道树木屏障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thick screen of trees hid the old house from the road.  一道浓密的树木屏障把老屋从路上遮住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>finale</b></td><td>/fɪˈnæli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>终场，结局</td><td>the grand finale<br>盛大的终场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra saved its loudest music for the grand finale.  乐团把最响亮的乐段留给了盛大的终场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coda</td><td>/ˈkoʊdə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终曲</td><td>a triumphant coda<br>辉煌的终曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The symphony ends with a quiet and unexpectedly gentle coda.  这部交响曲以安静而出乎意料温柔的尾声结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prologue</td><td>/ˈproʊlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序言</td><td>the prologue to the play<br>该剧的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prologue to the play explains how the bitter feud began.  该剧的序言说明了这场激烈仇怨的由来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>finesse</b></td><td>/fɪˈnes/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>娴熟技巧；（巧妙地）躲避</td><td>handle it with finesse<br>娴熟巧妙地处理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the awkward question with real finesse and everyone relaxed.  她娴熟巧妙地处理了那个尴尬的问题，大家都放松了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adeptness</del></td><td>/əˈdɛptnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>熟练，擅长</td><td>adeptness at negotiation<br>谈判技巧娴熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her adeptness at negotiation saved the company millions of dollars.  她娴熟的谈判技巧为公司节省了数百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adroitness</del></td><td>/əˈdrɔɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灵巧，机敏</td><td>political adroitness<br>政治手腕灵巧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He handled the hostile crowd with remarkable adroitness.  他极为机敏地应对了充满敌意的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cleverness</td><td>/ˈklevərnəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>聪明，机巧</td><td>sheer cleverness<br>纯粹的机巧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puzzle's design shows real cleverness rather than mere difficulty.  这个谜题的设计显示出真正的机巧，而非单纯的难度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dexterity</td><td>/dekˈsterəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灵巧，敏捷</td><td>manual dexterity<br>手的灵巧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Surgery demands great manual dexterity and very steady nerves.  外科手术需要极高的手部灵巧和极稳的神经。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proficiency</td><td>/prəˈfɪʃənsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>精通，熟练</td><td>proficiency in English<br>英语熟练</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The job requires proficiency in at least two foreign languages.  这份工作要求精通至少两门外语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avoid</td><td>/əˈvɔɪd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，回避</td><td>avoid the issue<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He carefully avoided answering the reporter's most difficult question.  他小心地避开了记者最难的那个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dodge</td><td>/dɑːdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>躲避</td><td>dodge the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister managed to dodge the question three times in one interview.  部长在一次采访中三次成功回避了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eschew</td><td>/ɪsˈtʃuː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刻意避开；戒绝</td><td>eschew violence<br>戒绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks eschew luxury and live on whatever the village offers.  僧侣们避开奢华，靠村民所给的一切生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evade</td><td>/ɪˈveɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>evade the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness evaded every question about the missing money.  证人回避了所有关于失踪钱款的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shun</td><td>/ʃʌn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避开，避免</td><td>shun public attention<br>避开公众关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The writer chose to shun public attention after winning the prize.  那位作家获奖后选择避开公众关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 awkwardness</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərdnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>笨拙；尴尬</td><td>social awkwardness<br>社交上的笨拙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His awkwardness with strangers faded after a few minutes.  他面对陌生人的笨拙在几分钟后消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ineptitude</td><td>/ɪˈneptɪtuːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无能，笨拙</td><td>political ineptitude<br>政治上的无能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failure exposed the ineptitude of the entire management team.  这次失败暴露了整个管理团队的无能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 gaucherie</del></td><td>/ˌɡoʊʃəˈri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>笨拙，不懂社交礼数</td><td>youthful gaucherie<br>年轻人的笨拙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He apologized for the gaucherie of arriving without an invitation.  他为未受邀就到场的失礼道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ungainliness</del></td><td>/ʌnˈɡeɪnlinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>笨拙，不灵活</td><td>the ungainliness of youth<br>少年人的笨拙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The colt's ungainliness disappeared the moment it began to run.  小马驹一跑起来，笨拙就消失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confront</td><td>/kənˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>直接对抗，直面</td><td>confront the problem<br>直面问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later you must confront the problem directly.  你早晚必须直面这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>finicky</b></td><td>/ˈfɪnɪki/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的；过分讲究的</td><td>finicky about food<br>对食物很挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child is finicky about food and refuses most vegetables.  这孩子对食物很挑剔，多数蔬菜都不吃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demanding</td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fastidious</td><td>/fæˈstɪdiəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，极仔细的，追求完美的</td><td>fastidious about details<br>对细节极挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fastidious about details and rereads every line twice.  她对细节极为挑剔，每一行都要读两遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fussy</td><td>/ˈfʌsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，过分讲究的</td><td>fussy about small details<br>对小细节很挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old tailor is fussy about the smallest details.  这位老裁缝对最小的细节都很挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nice</td><td>/ˈnaɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>规矩得体的；令人愉快的</td><td>nice manners<br>得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a nice boy who never gives his parents trouble.  他是个规矩的男孩，从不给父母添麻烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particular</td><td>/pərˈtɪkjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>详细的；特定的；讲究的</td><td>a particular account of events<br>对事件的详细说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a particular account of every repair the house needed.  他详细说明了房子所需的每一项修缮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undemanding</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>要求不高的，轻松的</td><td>an undemanding course<br>要求不高的课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chose an undemanding role so she could rest more.  她选了个轻松的角色以便多休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfussy</td><td>/ʌnˈfʌsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不挑剔的，不讲究的</td><td>an unfussy eater<br>不挑食的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an unfussy traveler who can sleep anywhere.  她是个不讲究的旅行者，在哪都能睡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flaccid</b></td><td>/ˈflæsɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不结实的，松弛的</td><td>flaccid muscles<br>松弛的肌肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks in bed left his leg muscles flaccid and weak.  数周卧床使他的腿部肌肉松弛无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 droopy</td><td>/ˈdruːpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>下垂的，松垂的</td><td>droopy eyelids<br>下垂的眼皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog's droopy ears made it look permanently sad.  这只狗下垂的耳朵让它看起来总是很忧伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 floppy</td><td>/ˈflɑːpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松软的，松垂的</td><td>a floppy hat<br>软塌塌的帽子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wore a floppy straw hat to block the sun.  她戴着一顶软塌塌的草帽遮阳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lank</td><td>/læŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细长瘦弱的；不僵硬的，柔软松弛的</td><td>lank hair<br>柔软下垂的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lank hair fell over his forehead whenever he leaned forward.  他柔软下垂的头发每次前倾时都垂到额前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loose</td><td>/ˈluːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，放开；松的</td><td>loose the dogs<br>放开猎狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper loosed the hawk and watched it climb into the sky.  饲养员放开那只鹰，看它飞向天空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slack</td><td>/slæk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的；疏忽的，大意的</td><td>slack about deadlines<br>对截止日期松懈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager was slack about deadlines and lost several clients.  新经理对截止日期很松懈，因此丢了好几个客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sturdy</td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flag</b></td><td>/flæɡ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>his energy began to flag<br>他的精力开始衰减</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His energy began to flag after the twentieth mile.  跑到第二十英里后他的精力开始衰减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decline</td><td>/dɪˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拒绝；下降，衰退</td><td>decline an invitation<br>谢绝邀请</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She politely declined the invitation to speak at the ceremony.  她礼貌地谢绝了在典礼上发言的邀请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 emaciate</del></td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使消瘦，使憔悴</td><td>emaciate the patient's body<br>使病人身体消瘦衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of fever had emaciated the patient's body almost beyond recognition.  数月高烧使病人的身体消瘦衰弱得几乎认不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fade</td><td>/ˈfeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，消退；褪色</td><td>hopes began to fade<br>希望开始消退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hopes of rescue began to fade after three days.  三天后他们获救的希望开始消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fail</td><td>/ˈfeɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，衰竭；失败</td><td>his strength failed<br>他体力衰竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eyesight began to fail in his early seventies.  他七十出头时视力开始衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languish</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>languish in a damp cell<br>在潮湿牢房中日渐衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners languished in damp cells for months without proper food.  囚犯们在潮湿的牢房里日渐衰弱，数月没有适当的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sag</td><td>/ˈsæɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>下垂，下陷；衰退</td><td>the old roof sags badly<br>旧屋顶严重下陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mattress sagged in the middle after years of use.  用了多年后，床垫中间下陷了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wilt</td><td>/wɪlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>精神萎靡，憔悴</td><td>wilt in the heat<br>在酷热中萎靡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The runners began to wilt in the heat of the final mile.  跑到最后一英里的酷热中，选手们开始萎靡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thrive</td><td>/ˈθraɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴旺，繁荣，茁壮成长</td><td>thrive in adversity<br>在逆境中兴旺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small shops thrive when the local economy is healthy.  当地经济健康时，小店就会兴旺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flamboyant</b></td><td>/flæmˈbɔɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>艳丽夺目的，炫耀的，充满装饰的</td><td>a flamboyant style<br>浮夸炫目的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The singer wore a flamboyant coat covered in mirrors and feathers.  歌手穿着一件缀满镜片和羽毛的艳丽夺目的外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaring</td><td>/ˈflerɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耀眼张扬的；摇曳燃烧的</td><td>flaring colors<br>耀眼的色彩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer's flaring costume drew every eye in the theater.  舞者张扬耀眼的服装吸引了剧场里所有的目光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 florid</td><td>/ˈflɔːrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的，花哨的</td><td>florid prose<br>辞藻华丽的散文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic mocked the florid prose of the young novelist.  评论家嘲讽这位年轻小说家辞藻华丽的文风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garish</td><td>/ˈɡerɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于鲜艳的，过于张扬的</td><td>garish colors<br>过于鲜艳的颜色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lobby was painted in garish colors that hurt the eyes.  大堂被漆成刺眼的过于鲜艳的颜色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ornate</td><td>/ɔrˈneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>华丽的，装饰繁复的</td><td>ornate decoration<br>繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hall's ornate ceiling took two years to paint.  大厅华丽繁复的天花板画了两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ostentatious</td><td>/ˌɑːstenˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫耀卖弄的</td><td>ostentatious display of wealth<br>炫富的排场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ostentatious diamond watch drew stares from everyone in the modest room.  他炫耀的钻石表引来了朴素房间里所有人的注视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 showy</td><td>/ˈʃoʊi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫耀的，花哨的</td><td>a showy display<br>招摇的展示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drives a showy sports car to impress his clients.  他开一辆花哨的跑车来打动客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flatter</b></td><td>/ˈflætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉承；讨好；谄媚</td><td>flatter the boss<br>奉承老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flattered the boss all morning hoping for a promotion.  他整个上午奉承老板，希望得到升职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 adulate</del></td><td>/ˈædʒəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>极度谄媚，拍马屁</td><td>adulate a celebrity<br>对名人极力吹捧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters adulated the young singer after her first hit.  记者们在这位年轻歌手第一首热门歌后对她大加吹捧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 blandish</del></td><td>/ˈblændɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>奉承，讨好，哄诱</td><td>blandish the boss<br>讨好奉承老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He blandished the officials until they signed the permit.  他一路奉承官员，直到他们签下许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliment</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞美，称赞；恭维</td><td>pay someone a compliment<br>赞美某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He paid her a warm compliment on her new painting.  他对她的新画作说了一句诚挚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 court</td><td>/kɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>追求，献殷勤；法庭</td><td>court public favor<br>讨好公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate spent the summer courting voters in small rural towns.  这位候选人整个夏天都在乡村小镇讨好选民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 massage</td><td>/məˈsɑːʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉承，迎合；按摩</td><td>massage his ego<br>迎合他的自尊心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The consultant massaged the director's ego to win approval.  顾问奉承主管的自尊心以换取批准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overpraise</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈpreɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>过分赞扬</td><td>overpraise a mediocre film<br>过分赞誉平庸电影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics tend to overpraise any novel by a famous author.  评论家往往对名作家的任何小说都过分赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stroke</td><td>/stroʊk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉承，抚慰</td><td>stroke his vanity<br>奉承他的虚荣心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She knew exactly how to stroke her boss's vanity.  她非常清楚该如何奉承老板的虚荣心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wheedle</td><td>/ˈwiːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（用花言巧语）诱惑，哄骗</td><td>wheedle money out of him<br>哄他掏出钱来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wheedled her father into lending her the old car.  她哄着父亲把那辆旧车借给她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abuse</td><td>/əˈbjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辱骂，抨击；不正当或不合理使用</td><td>abuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of abusing his position for profit.  他被指控滥用职位谋利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flaw</b></td><td>/flɔː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>瑕疵，缺点；降低，破坏，使不完美</td><td>a fatal flaw<br>致命的缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One small flaw in the design caused the bridge to fail.  设计中一个小缺陷导致桥梁失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blight</td><td>/blaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）枯萎；损害</td><td>blight the harvest<br>使收成枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain and disease can blight the harvest in a single week.  暴雨和病害能在一周之内毁掉收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blotch</td><td>/ˈblɑːtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>斑点，污渍；使起斑</td><td>red blotches on skin<br>皮肤上的红斑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ink blotches covered the corner of the carefully written letter.  墨渍污了那封精心书写信件的一角。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defect</td><td>/ˈdiːfekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>缺陷，瑕疵；叛逃</td><td>a manufacturing defect<br>制造缺陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny defect in the wiring caused the entire system failure.  线路上一个微小缺陷导致了整个系统故障。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fault</td><td>/ˈfɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，过错；断层</td><td>find fault with<br>挑剔，找毛病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His only fault is that he never admits being wrong.  他唯一的缺点是从不承认自己错了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperfection</td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈfekʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>瑕疵，缺陷</td><td>a minor imperfection<br>细小的瑕疵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tiny imperfection in the glass ruined the whole lens.  玻璃中一个微小的瑕疵毁了整枚镜片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mar</td><td>/mɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>破坏，削弱</td><td>mar the celebration<br>破坏庆典气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A careless remark at dinner managed to mar an otherwise perfect evening.  餐桌上一句轻率的话毁掉了本该完美的一晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scar</td><td>/ˈskɑːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>伤疤，创痕；留下伤痕</td><td>leave a deep scar<br>留下深深伤疤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The accident left a thin scar above his left eyebrow.  那次事故在他左眉上方留下一道细疤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spot</td><td>/ˈspɑːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>污点，斑点；地点</td><td>a spot on the shirt<br>衬衫上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grease spot on the sleeve ruined his only white shirt.  袖子上的油污弄坏了他唯一的白衬衫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blemish</td><td>/ˈblemɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺点，污点；损害，玷污</td><td>a blemish on his record<br>他履历上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One late payment left a small blemish on his credit record.  一次逾期付款在他的信用记录上留下了小小的污点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compromise</td><td>/ˈkɑːmprəmaɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>妥协，折中；危害</td><td>reach a compromise<br>达成妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After long debate the committee reached a workable compromise.  经过长时间辩论，委员会达成了可行的妥协。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cripple</td><td>/ˈkrɪpəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使严重受损，削弱</td><td>cripple the economy<br>严重削弱经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike crippled the city's transport system for a week.  罢工使该市交通系统瘫痪了一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harm</td><td>/ˈhɑːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伤害，损害</td><td>harm the environment<br>损害环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pesticide runoff harms the fish living in the nearby stream.  农药径流危害了附近溪流中的鱼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurt</td><td>/ˈhɜːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>伤害，使疼痛</td><td>hurt his feelings<br>伤害他的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your careless comment clearly hurt her feelings at the dinner.  你随口的评论显然在晚餐上伤了她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impair</td><td>/ɪmˈper/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>损害，削弱</td><td>impair his judgment<br>削弱他的判断力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep seriously impairs judgment and slows your reactions.  睡眠不足严重损害判断力并减慢反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 injure</td><td>/ˈɪndʒər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使受伤，伤害</td><td>injure his knee<br>伤到他的膝盖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He injured his knee while running downhill on wet stones.  他在湿滑石头上跑下坡时伤了膝盖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoil</td><td>/ˈspɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>损坏，破坏；溺爱</td><td>spoil the surprise<br>破坏惊喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain spoiled the picnic we had planned for weeks.  大雨破坏了我们筹划数周的野餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitiate</td><td>/ˈvɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，损害；使无效</td><td>vitiate an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single false statement can vitiate the entire contract.  一句虚假陈述就能使整份合同失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fix</td><td>/ˈfɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，解决；固定</td><td>fix the leaking pipe<br>修好漏水的管子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neighbor fixed the leaking pipe before the floor was ruined.  邻居在地板毁坏前修好了漏水的管子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mend</td><td>/ˈmend/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>纠正，改正；修补</td><td>mend his ways<br>改正他的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He promised to mend his ways after the second stern warning.  第二次严厉警告后他承诺改正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repair</td><td>/rɪˈper/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>修理，修补</td><td>repair the roof<br>修补屋顶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers repaired the roof just before the rainy season began.  工人们在雨季开始前修好了屋顶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revamp</td><td>/riˈvæmp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>改进，翻新，改造</td><td>revamp the website<br>改版网站</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company revamped its website to attract younger overseas customers.  公司改版网站以吸引更年轻的海外顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fledgling</b></td><td>/ˈfledʒlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>新手</td><td>a fledgling company<br>新兴的公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fledgling airline struggled through its very first winter.  这家新兴航空公司艰难度过了第一个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprentice</td><td>/əˈprentəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>学徒，新手</td><td>a carpenter's apprentice<br>木匠学徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He started as an apprentice and now owns the shop.  他从学徒做起，现在拥有这家店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beginner</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪnər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>初学者，新手</td><td>a complete beginner<br>完全的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This course is designed for the absolute beginner.  这门课程是为完全的新手设计的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freshman</td><td>/ˈfreʃmən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大一新生；新手</td><td>a freshman senator<br>新任参议员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a freshman he knew almost nobody on campus.  作为大一新生，他在校园里几乎谁也不认识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neophyte</td><td>/ˈniːəfaɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>初学者，新手</td><td>a neophyte in politics<br>政界的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a neophyte in politics, he underestimated how slowly legislation actually moves.  作为政界的新手，他低估了立法进程实际有多缓慢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 novice</td><td>/ˈnɑːvɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>新手，初学者</td><td>a complete novice<br>彻底的新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A complete novice, he had never held a chisel before that afternoon.  他是个彻底的新手，那天下午之前从没握过凿子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tyro</td><td>/ˈtaɪroʊ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>新手，业余爱好者</td><td>a tyro at chess<br>国际象棋新手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a tyro at chess, he lost every game that afternoon.  作为国际象棋新手，他那天下午每局都输了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recruit</td><td>/rəˈkruːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>新成员，新兵；招募</td><td>a raw recruit<br>毫无经验的新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every new recruit spends six weeks in basic training.  每名新兵都要接受六周基础训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rookie</td><td>/ˈrʊki/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>新兵；新手；菜鸟</td><td>a rookie mistake<br>新手才犯的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rookie made a nervous throw that cost his team the game.  那名新手一次紧张的传球让球队输掉了比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veteran</td><td>/ˈvetərən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>老兵；有丰富经验的人</td><td>a veteran journalist<br>资深记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A veteran journalist, she has covered four wars and six elections.  作为资深记者，她报道过四场战争和六次选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fleet</b></td><td>/fliːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的</td><td>fleet of foot<br>脚步迅捷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fleet young messenger crossed the valley before dark.  这位迅捷的年轻信使天黑前穿过了山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brisk</td><td>/brɪsk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满生机的，有活力的</td><td>a brisk walk<br>快步散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brisk walk before breakfast leaves me alert all morning.  早餐前快走一趟能让我整个上午都很清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expeditious</td><td>/ˌekspəˈdɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速而高效的</td><td>an expeditious response<br>迅速的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee promised an expeditious review of the complaint.  委员会承诺迅速审查这项投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fast</td><td>/fæst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的，快的</td><td>a fast runner<br>跑得快的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a fast worker who finishes every job ahead of schedule.  他做事很快，每项工作都提前完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hasty</td><td>/ˈheɪsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，仓促的；轻率的</td><td>a hasty judgment<br>轻率的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A hasty decision made in anger often costs far more later.  盛怒之下作出的轻率决定往往日后代价更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nimble</td><td>/ˈnɪmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，灵活的</td><td>nimble fingers<br>灵活的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nimble fingers tied the knot in one second.  她灵活的手指一秒钟就打好了结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quick</td><td>/ˈkwɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快的，迅速的</td><td>a quick decision<br>迅速的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quick decision saved the patient's life that night.  一个迅速的决定在那晚救了病人的命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapid</td><td>/ˈræpəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的，快速的</td><td>rapid economic growth<br>迅速的经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city saw rapid change after the railway finally arrived.  铁路终于通达后这座城市迅速变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rattling</td><td>/ˈrætlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>飞快的；非常好的</td><td>a rattling pace<br>飞快的速度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The train moved at a rattling pace across the plain.  火车以飞快的速度穿过平原。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speedy</td><td>/ˈspiːdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快速的，迅速的</td><td>a speedy recovery<br>迅速康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone wished her a speedy recovery after the operation.  手术后大家都祝她迅速康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swift</td><td>/ˈswɪft/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的，敏捷的</td><td>a swift response<br>迅速的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swift decision saved the patient a great deal of pain.  她迅速的决定让病人少受许多痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slow</td><td>/ˈsloʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缓慢的，迟缓的</td><td>slow progress<br>缓慢的进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Progress on the bridge has been painfully slow this year.  今年大桥的进展慢得令人痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flexible</b></td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adaptable</td><td>/əˈdæptəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>适应力强的；可改编的</td><td>a highly adaptable species<br>适应力极强的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crows are adaptable birds that thrive even in crowded cities.  乌鸦适应力很强，连拥挤的城市里也能繁盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjustable</td><td>/əˈdʒʌstəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可调节的</td><td>an adjustable office chair<br>可调节的办公椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The desk has adjustable legs so it suits both children and adults.  这张桌子的腿可调节，大人小孩都合用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 alterable</del></td><td>/ˈɔltərəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可改变的，可变更的</td><td>an alterable schedule<br>可变更的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract terms are alterable only with written consent.  合同条款只有经书面同意才可变更。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 changeable</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易变的，可改变的</td><td>changeable weather<br>多变的天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weather in April is notoriously changeable here.  这里四月的天气以多变著称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elastic</td><td>/ɪˈlæstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有弹性的；灵活的</td><td>an elastic waistband<br>有弹性的腰带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These trousers have an elastic waistband, so they fit almost anyone.  这条裤子有松紧腰带，几乎任何人都能穿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluid</td><td>/ˈfluːɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>易变的，不固定的；流体</td><td>a fluid situation<br>变动不定的局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule remains fluid until the visas finally arrive.  在签证最终到手前，日程仍不固定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malleable</td><td>/ˈmæliəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可塑的</td><td>malleable metals like gold<br>像黄金一样可塑的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gold and other malleable metals can be hammered into thin sheets.  黄金和其他易延展的金属可以被锤成薄片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modifiable</td><td>/ˈmɑdəˌfaɪəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可修改的，可调整的</td><td>a modifiable design<br>可修改的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All settings in the program are modifiable by the user.  程序中所有设置都可由用户修改。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variable</td><td>/ˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可变的，多变的</td><td>variable costs<br>可变成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Winds in the strait are variable and often dangerous.  海峡里的风多变且常常危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manageable</td><td>/ˈmænɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易管理的，好对付的</td><td>a manageable workload<br>可应付的工作量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Split the task into smaller, manageable pieces first.  先把任务拆成较小、好处理的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fixed</td><td>/ˈfɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定的，不变的</td><td>a fixed monthly rate<br>固定的月费率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tenants pay a fixed rent for the first three years.  租户前三年支付固定租金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immutable</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可变的</td><td>an immutable law of nature<br>不可变的自然法则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gravity is an immutable law that no clever argument can suspend.  引力是不可变的法则，任何巧辩都无法使它失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invariable</td><td>/ɪnˈveriəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不变的，恒定的</td><td>an invariable routine<br>一成不变的常规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His morning routine has been invariable for twenty years.  他的晨间习惯二十年来一成不变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 mulish</del></td><td>/ˈmjuːlɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>mulish refusal to listen<br>固执地不肯听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mulish refusal to listen frustrated everyone on the committee.  他固执地拒绝倾听，让委员会所有人都很沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flinch</b></td><td>/flɪntʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>畏缩，退缩；回避</td><td>flinch from the truth<br>不敢面对真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never flinched from telling his patients the difficult truth.  他从不回避向病人讲述艰难的真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cringe</td><td>/ˈkrɪndʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>卑躬屈膝；畏缩</td><td>cringe before the boss<br>在老板面前卑躬屈膝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The servant cringed whenever the duke raised his voice.  公爵一提高声音，仆人就畏缩起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quail</td><td>/kweɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>胆怯，畏缩</td><td>quail at the thought<br>一想到就发怵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even veteran soldiers quail at the thought of another winter campaign.  连老兵一想到又一场冬季战役也会畏缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recoil</td><td>/rɪˈkɔɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退却，畏缩</td><td>recoil in horror<br>惊恐地退缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She recoiled in horror when the lamp revealed the broken door.  灯光照出被砸坏的门时，她惊恐地退缩了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrink</td><td>/ʃrɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（在数量或价值方面）降低，减小</td><td>shrink in value<br>价值缩水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The value of the old coins began to shrink after the scandal.  丑闻之后，那些旧币的价值开始缩水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wince</td><td>/wɪns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>畏缩</td><td>wince at the pain<br>因疼痛而龇牙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She winced as the nurse cleaned the deep cut on her arm.  护士清洗她手臂上的深口子时，她疼得直畏缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 face</td><td>/ˈfeɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>面对，正视；脸，面</td><td>face the truth<br>正视事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later she must face the truth about the debt.  她迟早得正视这笔债务的真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meet</td><td>/miːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的</td><td>a meet response<br>合适的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A public apology seemed the only meet response to such a serious blunder.  公开道歉似乎是对如此严重失误唯一合适的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flippancy</b></td><td>/ˈflɪpənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻率；轻浮无礼</td><td>treat it with flippancy<br>以轻浮态度对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippancy during the memorial upset the grieving family.  他在悼念仪式上的轻浮无礼让悲伤的家人很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 facetiousness</del></td><td>/fəˈsiːʃəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不合时宜的诙谐，轻浮</td><td>unwelcome facetiousness<br>不受欢迎的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His facetiousness at the funeral offended nearly everyone present.  他在葬礼上的轻浮玩笑几乎冒犯了所有在场者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 flightiness</del></td><td>/ˈflaɪtinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，反复无常</td><td>youthful flightiness<br>年轻人的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her flightiness made her a poor choice for the job.  她的轻浮反复使她不适合这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frivolousness</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪvələsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，无聊琐碎</td><td>sheer frivolousness<br>纯粹的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the frivolousness of the entire lawsuit.  法官批评了整场诉讼的无聊琐碎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frothiness</del></td><td>/ˈfrɔːθinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，浅薄；起泡沫</td><td>the frothiness of the show<br>这节目的浅薄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics mocked the frothiness of the summer blockbuster.  评论家嘲笑这部暑期大片的浅薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 levity</td><td>/ˈlevəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮</td><td>a moment of levity<br>一时的轻松打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A moment of levity relieved the tension in the crowded waiting room.  一时的轻松打趣缓解了拥挤候诊室里的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silliness</td><td>/ˈsɪlinəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愚蠢，无聊可笑</td><td>childish silliness<br>孩子气的傻闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher tolerated a little silliness at the end of class.  老师容许下课前有点傻闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 earnestness</td><td>/ˈɜːrnəstnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>认真，诚挚</td><td>speak with earnestness<br>诚挚地讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He argued his case with disarming earnestness.  他以令人无法抗拒的诚挚陈述自己的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gravity</td><td>/ˈɡrævəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，庄重；重力</td><td>the gravity of the situation<br>形势的严峻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody seemed to grasp the gravity of the situation.  似乎没人明白形势的严峻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seriousness</td><td>/ˈsɪriəsnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，认真；严重性</td><td>the seriousness of the charge<br>指控的严重性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She explained the seriousness of the charge to her client.  她向委托人说明了指控的严重性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 soberness</del></td><td>/ˈsoʊbərnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，冷静；清醒</td><td>the soberness of his tone<br>他语气的严肃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soberness of his tone silenced the whole room.  他语气的严肃让整个房间安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solemnity</td><td>/səˈlemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>with due solemnity<br>以应有的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coronation was conducted with all the solemnity the occasion demanded.  加冕礼以这一场合所要求的全部庄严举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 solemnness</del></td><td>/ˈsɑləmnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>the solemnness of the oath<br>誓言的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The solemnness of the ceremony moved even the children.  仪式的庄严甚至打动了孩子们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flirt</b></td><td>/flɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>调情</td><td>flirt with the waiter<br>跟服务员调情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waitress cheerfully flirted with every single man in the restaurant.  女服务员愉快地和餐厅里每个单身男子调情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coquet</td><td>/koʊˈkɛt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>调情；玩弄，不认真对待</td><td>coquet with an idea<br>不认真地摆弄某想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He liked to coquet with dangerous ideas he never pursued.  他喜欢玩弄那些从不真正追求的危险想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frivol</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪvəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度光阴；轻浮对待</td><td>frivol away the afternoon<br>虚度整个下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They frivoled away the whole afternoon playing cards.  他们打牌虚度了整个下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toy</td><td>/tɔɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>草率或不认真地对待</td><td>toy with the idea<br>不认真地考虑该想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She toyed with the idea of moving abroad but never made real plans.  她曾不认真地考虑过移居国外，却从未真正计划过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trifle</td><td>/ˈtraɪfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>轻视，玩弄；小事</td><td>trifle with her feelings<br>玩弄她的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not trifle with her feelings just to amuse yourself.  不要为了取乐而玩弄她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flit</b></td><td>/flɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>轻快地飞过；掠过</td><td>flit from place to place<br>从一处快速掠到另一处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small birds flit from branch to branch searching for hidden insects.  小鸟在枝间轻快地飞来飞去寻找隐藏的昆虫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dance</td><td>/ˈdæns/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>轻快移动，跳动</td><td>dance across the water<br>在水面上跳动闪掠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sunlight danced across the surface of the lake.  阳光在湖面上跳动闪掠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dart</td><td>/dɑːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>猛冲，突然移动</td><td>dart across the road<br>猛冲过马路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A squirrel darted across the lawn and vanished up the old oak.  一只松鼠猛冲过草坪，蹿上老橡树不见了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleet</td><td>/fliːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的</td><td>fleet of foot<br>脚步迅捷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fleet young messenger crossed the valley before dark.  这位迅捷的年轻信使天黑前穿过了山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flick</td><td>/ˈflɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>轻快闪动；轻弹</td><td>flick through the pages<br>快速翻动书页</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She flicked through the magazine while waiting for the bus.  她等公交时快速翻动着杂志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flirt</td><td>/flɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>调情</td><td>flirt with the waiter<br>跟服务员调情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waitress cheerfully flirted with every single man in the restaurant.  女服务员愉快地和餐厅里每个单身男子调情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flutter</td><td>/ˈflʌtər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>飘动；快速拍翅</td><td>flutter in the breeze<br>在微风中飘动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flags flutter in the breeze above the crowded stadium entrance.  旗帜在拥挤的体育场入口上方随微风飘动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurry</td><td>/ˈhɜːri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙前行，赶紧</td><td>hurry along the corridor<br>匆忙穿过走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hurried along the corridor to catch the elevator.  他匆忙穿过走廊去赶电梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 zip</td><td>/ˈzɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>飞快移动；拉上拉链</td><td>zip past the window<br>飞快掠过窗前</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motorcycle zipped past the window and vanished.  一辆摩托车飞快掠过窗前，消失不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plod</td><td>/ˈplɑːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>沉重缓慢地走，苦干</td><td>plod through the mud<br>在泥地里沉重跋涉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired horses plodded through the deep mud.  疲惫的马匹在深泥中沉重跋涉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flock</b></td><td>/flɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>聚集，集体行动</td><td>flock to the beach<br>成群涌向海滩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Holidaymakers flock to the beach as soon as school ends.  学校一放假，度假者就涌向海滩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mob</td><td>/ˈmɑːb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>蜂拥围住；乌合之众</td><td>mob the singer<br>蜂拥围住歌手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans mobbed the singer as she left the stage.  歌手离开舞台时粉丝蜂拥围住了她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swarm</td><td>/ˈswɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>蜂拥，云集；一大群</td><td>swarm into the square<br>蜂拥进入广场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters swarmed into the square before the announcement.  记者们在宣布之前蜂拥进入广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 throng</td><td>/θrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>大量聚集</td><td>throng the streets<br>挤满街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fans thronged the streets outside the stadium hours before kickoff.  开赛前几小时，球迷就挤满体育场外的街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>florid</b></td><td>/ˈflɔːrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的，花哨的</td><td>florid prose<br>辞藻华丽的散文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic mocked the florid prose of the young novelist.  评论家嘲讽这位年轻小说家辞藻华丽的文风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bombastic</td><td>/bɑːmˈbæstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>夸大的，浮夸的</td><td>bombastic rhetoric<br>浮夸的辞令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bombastic speech impressed nobody in the audience.  他浮夸的演讲没有打动任何听众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flowery</td><td>/ˈflaʊəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的；多花的</td><td>flowery language<br>辞藻华丽的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter was written in absurdly flowery language.  这封信用了荒唐华丽的辞藻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grandiloquent</td><td>/ɡrænˈdɪləkwənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>夸张的，说大话的</td><td>grandiloquent promises<br>夸张的许诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters grew tired of his grandiloquent promises about reform.  选民厌倦了他关于改革的夸张许诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 magniloquent</del></td><td>/mæɡˈnɪləkwənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>夸张的，辞藻浮夸的</td><td>a magniloquent style<br>浮夸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The preface is written in a magniloquent, old-fashioned style.  这篇序言以浮夸而老派的风格写成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rhetorical</td><td>/rɪˈtɔːrɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>言辞华丽（却往往无内涵）的</td><td>empty rhetorical flourishes<br>空洞的华丽辞藻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was full of rhetorical flourishes but offered no concrete plan.  他的演讲满是华丽辞藻，却没有提出任何具体计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flounder</b></td><td>/ˈflaʊndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动，挣扎</td><td>flounder in the water<br>在水中挣扎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swimmer floundered in the cold water before help arrived.  救援到来之前，游泳者在冷水中挣扎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blunder</td><td>/ˈblʌndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>大错，失误；犯大错</td><td>a costly blunder<br>代价高昂的失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sending the letter unsigned was a costly blunder.  把信未签名就寄出是个代价高昂的失误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fumble</td><td>/ˈfʌmbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>笨拙地做</td><td>fumble for the keys<br>笨拙地摸找钥匙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fumbled for the keys in his dark coat pocket.  他在昏暗的外套口袋里笨拙地摸找钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lumber</td><td>/ˈlʌmbər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动；使负担（从而拖累）</td><td>lumber across the yard<br>笨拙地穿过院子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bear lumbered across the yard toward the garbage cans.  那头老熊笨拙地穿过院子朝垃圾桶走去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plod</td><td>/ˈplɑːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>沉重缓慢地走，苦干</td><td>plod through the mud<br>在泥地里沉重跋涉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired horses plodded through the deep mud.  疲惫的马匹在深泥中沉重跋涉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 struggle</td><td>/ˈstrʌɡəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>挣扎，费力地做；斗争</td><td>struggle to keep up<br>挣扎着跟上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He struggled to keep up with the rest of the class.  他挣扎着跟上班上其他同学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trudge</td><td>/trʌdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>吃力而笨拙地走</td><td>trudge through the snow<br>在雪中吃力行走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We trudged home through deep snow with our heavy canvas bags.  我们背着沉重的帆布包，在深雪中吃力地走回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glide</td><td>/ɡlaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻松地行动，轻松地通过</td><td>glide across the floor<br>轻盈地滑过地板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skaters glided across the frozen pond without making a single sound.  滑冰者们悄无声息地轻盈滑过结冰的池塘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slide</td><td>/ˈslaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>滑行，顺畅移动；下滑</td><td>slide into the room<br>悄然滑进房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She slid into the room without making any sound.  她悄无声息地滑进了房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flourish</b></td><td>/ˈflɜːrɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>茂盛；繁荣</td><td>flourish in warm climates<br>在温暖气候中繁茂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Olive trees flourish in the dry, sunny hills of southern Spain.  橄榄树在西班牙南部干燥向阳的山丘上长得茂盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
