<tr><td>↳近 bloom</td><td>/ˈbluːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>繁盛，兴旺；开花</td><td>bloom into a career<br>发展成一份事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hobby bloomed into a highly profitable business.  她的爱好蓬勃发展成了一门赚钱的生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burgeon</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>迅速成长扩大，蓬勃发展</td><td>a burgeoning market<br>迅速发展的市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's tech sector burgeoned after the new university opened.  新大学开办后，该市的科技产业迅速壮大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prosper</td><td>/ˈprɑːspər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>繁荣，兴旺，成功</td><td>prosper under new management<br>在新管理下兴旺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small bakery prospered once tourists discovered the town.  游客发现这个小镇后，那家小面包店就兴旺起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thrive</td><td>/ˈθraɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>兴旺，繁荣，茁壮成长</td><td>thrive in adversity<br>在逆境中兴旺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small shops thrive when the local economy is healthy.  当地经济健康时，小店就会兴旺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 languish</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>languish in a damp cell<br>在潮湿牢房中日渐衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners languished in damp cells for months without proper food.  囚犯们在潮湿的牢房里日渐衰弱，数月没有适当的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flout</b></td><td>/flaʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄性不理会，蔑视</td><td>flout the rules<br>蔑视规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drivers who flout the rules face heavy fines this year.  今年蔑视规则的司机将面临重罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gibe</td><td>/dʒaɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>嘲弄</td><td>gibe at his accent<br>嘲弄他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The other students gibed at his accent until he stopped speaking.  其他学生嘲弄他的口音，直到他不再开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sneer</td><td>/snɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（轻蔑地）嘲笑</td><td>sneer at his efforts<br>嘲笑他的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics sneered at his efforts, but he kept working on the design.  批评者嘲笑他的努力，他却继续完善设计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taunt</td><td>/tɔːnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>嘲弄，讥讽；挑衅</td><td>taunt the loser<br>嘲弄失败者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The winning boys taunted the loser until a teacher intervened.  获胜的男孩们不停嘲弄输者，直到老师出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revere</td><td>/rɪˈvɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（尤指对神的）尊崇，尊敬</td><td>revered as a hero<br>被尊崇为英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students in the village still revere the teacher who founded their school.  村里的学生仍然尊崇创办他们学校的那位老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 venerate</td><td>/ˈvenəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>尊敬</td><td>venerate their ancestors<br>崇敬他们的祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers still venerate their ancestors with offerings twice each year.  村民每年两次以供品崇敬他们的祖先。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flabby</b></td><td>/ˈflæbi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲软的，无力的</td><td>flabby arms<br>松软无力的手臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without exercise left his arms flabby and weak.  几个月不锻炼使他的手臂松软无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitated</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，衰弱的</td><td>debilitated by long illness<br>因久病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient looked pale and debilitated after months of treatment.  经过数月治疗，病人显得苍白虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effete</td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enervated</td><td>/ˈɛnərveɪtəd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，无力的</td><td>enervated by the heat<br>被暑热弄得虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long illness left him enervated and unusually pale.  长期患病使他虚弱且异常苍白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faint</td><td>/ˈfeɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；微弱的</td><td>a faint voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt faint after standing in the sun too long.  在太阳下站太久后她感到虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fragile</td><td>/ˈfrædʒl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的</td><td>a fragile vase<br>易碎的花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please pack the fragile vase in plenty of soft paper.  请用足够多的软纸把这个易碎的花瓶包好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frail</td><td>/freɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；脆弱的</td><td>a frail old woman<br>体弱的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A frail old woman waited alone at the crowded bus stop.  一位虚弱的老妇人独自等在拥挤的公交站。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infirm</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；不果断的，优柔寡断的</td><td>old and infirm<br>年老体弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother grew too infirm to climb the stairs without help.  她的祖母身体太虚弱，没人帮忙就爬不了楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sapped</td><td>/ˈsæpt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>力气被耗尽的，虚弱的</td><td>sapped of energy<br>精力被耗尽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team looked sapped after three games in one week.  一周三场比赛后球队看来精力耗尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mighty</td><td>/ˈmaɪti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强大的，有力的</td><td>a mighty river<br>浩荡的大河</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mighty oak had stood there for three centuries.  一棵巨大的橡树在那里矗立了三个世纪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 powerful</td><td>/ˈpaʊərfəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强大的，有力的</td><td>a powerful engine<br>强劲的发动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The telescope is powerful enough to show distant galaxies.  这台望远镜足够强大，能显示遥远的星系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stalwart</td><td>/ˈstɔːlwərt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定忠诚的；强壮的</td><td>a stalwart supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained a stalwart supporter of the project through every setback.  历经挫折，她始终是这个项目的坚定支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stout</td><td>/ˈstaʊt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，勇敢的；粗壮的</td><td>stout resistance<br>顽强的抵抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The townspeople put up a stout defense against the raiders.  镇民对袭击者进行了顽强的抵抗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fluent</b></td><td>/ˈfluːənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达流利的</td><td>fluent in three languages<br>精通三门语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fluent in three languages and translates for the court.  她精通三门语言，为法庭做翻译。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eloquent</td><td>/ˈeləkwənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>雄辩的，有说服力的</td><td>an eloquent speech<br>雄辩的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eloquent defense of the plan won several extra votes.  他对该计划雄辩的辩护多赢得了几张选票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silver-tongued</td><td>/ˌsɪlvər ˈtʌŋd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>口才好的，能言善辩的</td><td>a silver-tongued lawyer<br>能言善辩的律师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The silver-tongued salesman persuaded her to buy two cars.  能言善辩的销售员说服她买了两辆车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-spoken</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈspoʊkən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谈吐文雅的，口齿清晰的</td><td>a well-spoken young man<br>谈吐文雅的青年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The well-spoken candidate impressed everyone on the hiring panel.  谈吐文雅的候选人给招聘小组留下深刻印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inarticulate</td><td>/ˌɪnɑrˈtɪkjələt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达不清的，说不出话的</td><td>inarticulate with rage<br>气得说不出话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became inarticulate whenever the topic turned personal.  一旦话题转到私事，他就说不清话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ineloquent</del></td><td>/ɪnˈɛləkwənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不善言辞的，不流畅的</td><td>an ineloquent reply<br>拙于言辞的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ineloquent answer disappointed the audience expecting fireworks.  他拙于言辞的回答让期待精彩的听众失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unvocal</del></td><td>/ʌnˈvoʊkəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不发声的，不表态的</td><td>remain unvocal about it<br>对此不表态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The usually loud critics stayed unvocal about the scandal.  平时高声的批评者对这桩丑闻保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fluky</b></td><td>/ˈfluːki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侥幸的</td><td>a fluky victory<br>侥幸的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fluky coincidence put me indoors when the blizzard finally struck.  一个侥幸的巧合让暴风雪来袭时我正待在室内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortuitous</td><td>/fɔːrˈtuːɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的，碰巧的</td><td>a fortuitous meeting<br>一次偶然的相遇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fortuitous meeting at the airport changed both careers.  他们在机场的偶然相遇改变了两人的职业道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucky</td><td>/ˈlʌki/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幸运的</td><td>a lucky escape<br>幸运的脱险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had a lucky escape when the roof collapsed.  屋顶塌落时他们幸运地脱险了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 providential</td><td>/ˌprɑːvɪˈdenʃl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天意般的，幸运的</td><td>a providential rescue<br>天意般的获救</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their providential rescue came just minutes before the boat sank.  他们天意般的获救就发生在船沉前几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hapless</td><td>/ˈhæpləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倒霉的，不幸的</td><td>the hapless victim<br>倒霉的受害者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hapless traveler lost both his passport and wallet.  这位倒霉的旅客丢了护照和钱包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfortunate</td><td>/ʌnˈfɔːrtʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的，遗憾的</td><td>an unfortunate mistake<br>不幸的失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was unfortunate that the letter arrived a week too late.  遗憾的是那封信迟到了一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unlucky</td><td>/ʌnˈlʌki/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的，倒霉的</td><td>an unlucky guess<br>不走运的猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was unlucky to miss the last train by seconds.  他很倒霉，差几秒钟没赶上末班车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-fated</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈfeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>注定不幸的，命运多灾的</td><td>an ill-fated expedition<br>注定不幸的远征</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two men returned from that ill-fated expedition.  那次注定不幸的远征只有两人生还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>flush</b></td><td>/flʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、不及物动词</td><td>充裕的，有钱的；冲洗</td><td>flush with money<br>钱很充裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the bonus he was flush with money for months.  拿到奖金后他有好几个月钱都很充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abounding</td><td>/əˈbaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，大量的</td><td>abounding in fish<br>盛产鱼类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They found a river abounding in trout.  他们发现一条鳟鱼很多的河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundant</td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awash</td><td>/əˈwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（如洪水般）泛滥的</td><td>awash with cash<br>现金泛滥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small town was awash with cash during the oil boom.  石油繁荣时期，这个小镇资金充裕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fraught</td><td>/frɔːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满（危险）的；忧虑的</td><td>fraught with danger<br>充满危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountain route is fraught with danger in early spring.  早春时节这条山路危机四伏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lousy</td><td>/ˈlaʊzi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多得很的，充满的；糟糕的</td><td>lousy with tourists<br>游客多得很</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In August the town is lousy with tourists from every corner of Europe.  八月这座小镇挤满了来自欧洲各地的游客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 replete</td><td>/rɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满…的，富于…的</td><td>replete with errors<br>错误连篇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay was replete with spelling errors.  他的文章满是拼写错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swarming</td><td>/ˈswɔːrmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拥挤的，密集涌动的</td><td>swarming with tourists<br>挤满游客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market was swarming with shoppers on Saturday morning.  周六早上市场里挤满了购物者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeming</td><td>/ˈtiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，充满的</td><td>teeming with life<br>充满生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond was teeming with tadpoles in May.  五月池塘里满是蝌蚪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thronging</td><td>/ˈθrɑːŋɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>蜂拥而至的，拥挤的</td><td>thronging crowds<br>蜂拥的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thronging fans filled the stadium hours before the concert began.  蜂拥的粉丝在演唱会开始前几小时就挤满了体育场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irrigate</td><td>/ˈɪrɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>灌溉；冲洗</td><td>irrigate the wound<br>冲洗伤口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse carefully irrigated the wound before applying a clean bandage.  护士仔细冲洗了伤口，然后敷上干净的绷带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rinse</td><td>/ˈrɪns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗，漂洗</td><td>rinse the dishes<br>冲洗碗盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rinse the vegetables under cold running water first.  先用冷流水冲洗蔬菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sluice</td><td>/ˈsluːs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗；水闸</td><td>sluice the deck<br>冲洗甲板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sailors sluiced the deck with buckets of seawater.  水手们用一桶桶海水冲洗甲板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wash</td><td>/ˈwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>冲洗，洗涤</td><td>wash away the dirt<br>冲掉污垢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain washed away the dirt from the steps.  大雨把台阶上的污垢冲掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inadequate</td><td>/ɪnˈædəkwət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不充足的，不胜任的</td><td>inadequate funding<br>资金不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lighting in the room is completely inadequate.  房间的照明完全不够。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insufficient</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈfɪʃənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，不够的</td><td>insufficient evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evidence was insufficient to convict him of the crime.  证据不足，无法判定他有罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scant</td><td>/skænt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>scant attention<br>很少的关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report paid scant attention to the concerns of local residents.  报告对当地居民的关切几乎没有给予关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 short</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短缺的，不足的</td><td>short of money<br>缺钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are short of money until the end of the month.  到月底之前我们都缺钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fluster</b></td><td>/ˈflʌstər/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使慌乱，使紧张</td><td>get flustered easily<br>容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unexpected questions always fluster him during interviews.  面试中意外的问题总让他慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abash</del></td><td>/əˈbæʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬，使羞愧</td><td>be abashed by praise<br>因受赞扬而羞怯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked abashed when everyone applauded his clumsy speech.  当大家为他笨拙的演讲鼓掌时，他显得很窘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confound</td><td>/kənˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；证明为假，证伪</td><td>confound the critics<br>让批评者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The late victory confounded the critics who had predicted failure.  最后的胜利让预言失败的批评者困惑不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confuse</td><td>/kənˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；混淆</td><td>confuse the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague instructions confused nearly everyone in the training class.  含糊的说明让培训班几乎所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discomfit</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬；阻碍</td><td>discomfit an opponent<br>使对手窘迫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blunt question visibly discomfited the confident speaker.  这个直率的问题明显让自信的演讲者局促不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconcert</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈsɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使慌乱</td><td>disconcert the audience<br>使观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His long silences disconcerted everyone at the table.  他长时间的沉默让餐桌上所有人都不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discountenance</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkaʊntənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成；使窘迫</td><td>discountenance such behavior<br>不赞成这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee discountenanced any further spending on the project.  委员会不赞成该项目再有任何支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faze</td><td>/feɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使慌乱，使窘迫</td><td>nothing seems to faze him<br>似乎没什么能扰乱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The huge crowd did not faze the young pianist at all.  庞大的观众丝毫没让这位年轻钢琴家慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mortify</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使羞愧，使难堪</td><td>be mortified with embarrassment<br>羞愧得无地自容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was mortified to find her mistake printed in the report.  发现自己的错误印在报告里，她羞愧难当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonplus</del></td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈplʌs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使迷惑，使困窘</td><td>be completely nonplussed<br>完全不知所措</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her strange reply left the whole panel nonplussed.  她奇怪的回答让整个评审团都困惑不解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rattle</td><td>/ˈrætəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>发出咔嗒声；使慌乱</td><td>rattle the windows<br>把窗户震得作响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passing train rattled every window in the old house.  驶过的火车震得老屋的每扇窗都咔咔作响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foible</b></td><td>/ˈfɔɪbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小缺点</td><td>human foibles<br>人性的小缺点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel treats human foibles with warmth rather than scorn.  这部小说以温情而非嘲讽看待人性的小缺点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demerit</td><td>/diˈmerət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，过失</td><td>merits and demerits<br>优点与缺点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report lists the merits and demerits of each plan.  报告列出了每个方案的优点与缺点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dereliction</td><td>/ˌderəˈlɪkʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失职，怠忽</td><td>dereliction of duty<br>失职，怠忽职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was dismissed for a serious dereliction of duty.  该官员因严重失职被解职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 failing</td><td>/ˈfeɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，弱点</td><td>her one failing<br>她唯一的缺点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Impatience is his greatest failing as a teacher.  没有耐心是他作为教师最大的缺点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fault</td><td>/ˈfɔːlt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，过错；断层</td><td>find fault with<br>挑剔，找毛病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His only fault is that he never admits being wrong.  他唯一的缺点是从不承认自己错了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frailty</td><td>/ˈfreɪlti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>弱点；脆弱</td><td>human frailty<br>人性的弱点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel explores human frailty with unusual tenderness.  这部小说以罕见的温柔探讨人性的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shortcoming</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtˌkʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺点，不足</td><td>admit one's shortcomings<br>承认自身不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good leader admits his own shortcomings openly.  好的领导者会坦然承认自己的不足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sin</td><td>/ˈsɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>罪过，过失</td><td>confess one's sins<br>承认自己的罪过</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In her view, wasting food is a serious sin.  在她看来，浪费食物是严重的过错。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vice</td><td>/ˈvaɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶习，缺点</td><td>his only vice<br>他唯一的恶习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Smoking is his only vice, and he defends it fiercely.  抽烟是他唯一的恶习，他还极力维护它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 want</td><td>/ˈwɑːnt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>缺乏，短缺</td><td>for want of evidence<br>因缺乏证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project collapsed for want of steady funding and clear leadership.  项目因缺乏稳定资金和明确领导而失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weakness</td><td>/ˈwiːknəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>弱点，缺点</td><td>a weakness for sweets<br>对甜食的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her main weakness is a reluctance to delegate work.  她的主要弱点是不愿把工作交给别人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 merit</td><td>/ˈmerət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>优点，价值</td><td>the merits of the plan<br>该方案的优点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee debated the merits of the plan for hours.  委员会就该方案的优点讨论了数小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 virtue</td><td>/ˈvɜːrtʃu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>美德，德行；优点</td><td>a life of virtue<br>有德行的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is a virtue that few travelers keep at airports.  耐心是少有旅客能在机场保持的美德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foil</b></td><td>/fɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>挫败</td><td>foil a plot<br>挫败阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards foiled the plot before anyone was hurt.  警觉的警卫在有人受伤之前挫败了阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baffle</td><td>/ˈbæfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使疑惑</td><td>baffle the experts<br>使专家困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange symptoms continued to baffle the visiting experts.  这些奇怪的症状一直使来访的专家疑惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balk</td><td>/bɔːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>阻碍；不愿接受，拒绝</td><td>balk at the price<br>因价格而退缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buyers began to balk at the price after the third increase.  第三次涨价之后，买家开始因价格而退缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beat</td><td>/ˈbiːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>打，击；击败</td><td>beat him mercilessly<br>无情地打他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards beat the prisoner until the officer finally intervened.  卫兵殴打囚犯，直到那位军官出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 checkmate</td><td>/ˈtʃekˌmeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使受挫，彻底击败；将死</td><td>checkmate his opponent<br>彻底挫败对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clever amendment checkmated the governor's entire plan.  一项巧妙的修正案彻底挫败了州长的全部计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discomfit</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬；阻碍</td><td>discomfit an opponent<br>使对手窘迫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blunt question visibly discomfited the confident speaker.  这个直率的问题明显让自信的演讲者局促不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thwart</td><td>/θwɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，挫败</td><td>thwart an attempt<br>挫败一次尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards thwarted the escape attempt.  警觉的守卫挫败了这次越狱企图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forward</td><td>/ˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向前的；前部的</td><td>take a forward step<br>向前迈一步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leaned forward to hear the quiet speaker better.  他向前倾身以便听清声音轻的讲话者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foster</td><td>/ˈfɔːstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，鼓励，培养</td><td>foster cooperation<br>促进合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new program fosters cooperation between local schools and museums.  新项目促进当地学校与博物馆之间的合作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foment</b></td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brew</td><td>/ˈbruː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>酝酿，渐渐形成；酿造</td><td>trouble is brewing<br>麻烦正在酝酿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trouble had been brewing in the department for months.  部门里的麻烦已酝酿了好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ferment</td><td>/ˈfɜːrment/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>动乱，骚动；发酵</td><td>in a state of ferment<br>处于动荡之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole country was in a state of ferment before the election.  大选前全国处于动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incite</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>incite a riot<br>煽动暴乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with inciting a riot outside the county courthouse.  他被指控在县法院外煽动暴乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raise</td><td>/ˈreɪz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提出（问题）；举起，提高</td><td>raise an objection<br>提出反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He raised an objection just before the vote was taken.  就在投票前他提出了反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quash</td><td>/kwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；镇压，阻止</td><td>quash a conviction<br>撤销有罪判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeal court quashed his conviction last week.  上诉法院上周撤销了对他的定罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quell</td><td>/kwel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压制；使平静，使安静</td><td>quell the unrest<br>平息动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Troops were sent in to quell the growing unrest.  部队被派去平息日益扩大的动乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 quench</td><td>/kwentʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解（渴）；熄灭</td><td>quench one's thirst<br>解渴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A glass of cold lemonade quickly quenched my desperate thirst.  一杯冰镇柠檬水很快解了我的焦渴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 squash</td><td>/ˈskwɑːʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>镇压，压制；压扁</td><td>squash the rumor<br>压下谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company quickly squashed the rumor about layoffs.  公司迅速压下了关于裁员的谣言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foolproof</b></td><td>/ˈfuːlpruːf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>十分简单以至于不会失败的</td><td>a foolproof plan<br>万无一失的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers promised a foolproof plan, but the machine jammed anyway.  工程师承诺了万无一失的方案，机器却还是卡住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guaranteed</td><td>/ˌɡærənˈtiːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>保证的，必定的</td><td>a guaranteed result<br>有保证的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This method offers a guaranteed result within two weeks.  这种方法保证两周内出结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infallible</td><td>/ɪnˈfæləbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>绝无错误的；万无一失的</td><td>an infallible method<br>万无一失的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No editor is infallible, however careful he may be.  再怎么细心，编辑也不可能绝无错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 safe</td><td>/ˈseɪf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>安全的，可靠的</td><td>a safe choice<br>稳妥的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Taking the train is the safe choice in this weather.  这种天气坐火车是稳妥的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfailing</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无穷的，可靠的</td><td>unfailing patience<br>无穷的耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed unfailing patience with the youngest students.  她对最小的学生表现出无穷的耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fail-safe</td><td>/ˈfeɪlseɪf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有故障保险的，万无一失的</td><td>a fail-safe system<br>有故障保险的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reactor has a fail-safe system that shuts it down.  反应堆有一套故障保险系统可将其关闭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fallible</td><td>/ˈfæləbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可能出错的</td><td>a fallible memory<br>可能出错的记忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even the best experts are fallible, so check their figures.  即便最优秀的专家也可能出错，所以要核对他们的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>footloose</b></td><td>/ˈfʊtluːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无拘无束的，自由的</td><td>footloose and fancy-free<br>无拘无束，自由自在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After graduation he stayed footloose and traveled for two whole years.  毕业后他一直无拘无束，旅行了整整两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loose</td><td>/ˈluːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，放开；松的</td><td>loose the dogs<br>放开猎狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper loosed the hawk and watched it climb into the sky.  饲养员放开那只鹰，看它飞向天空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbound</td><td>/ʌnˈbaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受束缚的，自由的</td><td>unbound by tradition<br>不受传统束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young designers here feel unbound by any tradition.  这里的年轻设计师觉得不受任何传统束缚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unconfined</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈfaɪnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受限制的，无拘束的</td><td>unconfined joy<br>毫无拘束的喜悦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children ran about with unconfined delight.  孩子们毫无拘束地欢快奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfettered</td><td>/ʌnˈfetərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受束缚的，自由的</td><td>unfettered access<br>不受限制的访问权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters demanded unfettered access to the disaster site.  记者们要求不受限制地进入灾区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrestrained</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受约束的；无节制的</td><td>unrestrained spending<br>无节制的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of unrestrained spending finally ruined the company.  多年无节制的开支最终毁掉了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untrammeled</td><td>/ʌnˈtræməld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受约束的，自由的</td><td>untrammeled freedom<br>不受约束的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the untrammeled freedom of a long summer.  他享受着漫长夏天里不受约束的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bound</td><td>/baʊnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>界限；决心的，一定会的</td><td>within the bounds of law<br>在法律范围内</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her curiosity knew no bounds when she entered the old library.  她走进那座旧图书馆时，好奇心毫无边界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confined</td><td>/kənˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭小的，受限制的</td><td>a confined space<br>狭小的空间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Working in such a confined space made the repair extremely difficult.  在如此狭小的空间作业使维修极为困难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 restrained</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，克制的</td><td>restrained by rules<br>受规则约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His restrained reply hid how angry he actually felt inside.  他克制的回答掩盖了他内心真正的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tied</td><td>/ˈtaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被束缚的，受牵制的</td><td>tied to the desk<br>被困在办公桌前</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt tied to the desk from morning until night.  她觉得自己从早到晚被困在办公桌前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forbearance</b></td><td>/fɔːrˈberəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>克制；忍耐</td><td>show great forbearance<br>表现出极大的忍耐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher showed great forbearance with the noisy class.  老师对吵闹的班级表现出极大的忍耐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patience</td><td>/ˈpeɪʃəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>耐心，忍耐</td><td>lose one's patience<br>失去耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He finally lost his patience with the noisy neighbors.  他终于对吵闹的邻居失去了耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperance</td><td>/ˈtempərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>节制，自我克制</td><td>temperance in all things<br>凡事节制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old doctor preached temperance in eating and drinking.  这位老医生倡导饮食节制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tolerance</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>容忍，宽容</td><td>tolerance for dissent<br>对异见的容忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school shows little tolerance for cheating of any kind.  学校对任何作弊都毫不容忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toleration</td><td>/ˌtɑːləˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>容忍，宽容（尤指对信仰）</td><td>religious toleration<br>宗教宽容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The act established religious toleration throughout the colony.  该法令在整个殖民地确立了宗教宽容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impatience</td><td>/ɪmˈpeɪʃəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不耐烦，急躁</td><td>a gesture of impatience<br>不耐烦的手势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His impatience with slow drivers is well known.  他对开车慢的人不耐烦是众所周知的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forebear</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrber/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>祖先</td><td>our distant forebears<br>我们遥远的祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our distant forebears crossed these high mountains on foot long ago.  我们遥远的祖先很久以前徒步穿越了这些高山。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ancestor</td><td>/ˈænsestər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>祖先，祖宗</td><td>trace one's ancestors<br>追溯祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ancestors came to this valley two centuries ago.  她的祖先两百年前来到这个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ascendant</td><td>/əˈsendənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有影响力的，主宰的</td><td>in the ascendant<br>处于上升地位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reformers were ascendant in the party for nearly a decade.  改革派在党内近十年处于主宰地位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 father</td><td>/ˈfɑːðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>父亲；创始人，先驱</td><td>the father of modern physics<br>现代物理学之父</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Galileo is often called the father of modern science.  伽利略常被称为现代科学之父。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grandfather</td><td>/ˈɡrændfɑːðər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>祖父，外祖父</td><td>my late grandfather<br>我已故的祖父</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather built this house with his own hands.  我祖父亲手建造了这座房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 primogenitor</del></td><td>/ˌpraɪmoʊˈdʒɛnətər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>最早的祖先，始祖</td><td>the primogenitor of the clan<br>该氏族的始祖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Legend names a wandering soldier as the family's primogenitor.  传说称一名流浪士兵为这家族的始祖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 progenitor</td><td>/proʊˈdʒenɪtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>祖先；先驱，前身</td><td>the progenitor of jazz<br>爵士乐的先驱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This machine was the progenitor of every modern computer.  这台机器是所有现代计算机的前身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 descendant</td><td>/dɪˈsendənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后代，子孙</td><td>a direct descendant<br>直系后代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She claims to be a direct descendant of the poet.  她自称是这位诗人的直系后代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 offspring</td><td>/ˈɔːfsˌprɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后代，子女</td><td>care for their offspring<br>照顾它们的后代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Penguins take turns feeding their hungry offspring.  企鹅轮流喂养饥饿的幼崽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forestall</b></td><td>/fɔːrˈstɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止，抢先防止</td><td>forestall criticism<br>预先化解批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered early to forestall further criticism.  她提前作答以预先阻止更多批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avert</td><td>/əˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，防止；转移（目光）</td><td>avert a disaster<br>避免灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Quick action by the crew averted a serious accident.  船员的迅速行动避免了一场严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deter</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吓住，威慑</td><td>deter potential criminals<br>威慑潜在罪犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Better street lighting may deter thieves from working in this neighborhood.  更好的街道照明或许能威慑小偷不在这一带作案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obviate</td><td>/ˈɑːbvieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排除，使不必要</td><td>obviate the need for<br>使……不再必要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short online form would obviate the need for three separate interviews.  一份简短的在线表格就能使三次单独面谈不再必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preclude</td><td>/prɪˈkluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止</td><td>preclude further discussion<br>阻止进一步讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lack of funding does not preclude us from starting the project.  资金不足并不能阻止我们启动这个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prevent</td><td>/prɪˈvent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻止，防止</td><td>prevent an accident<br>防止事故发生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple handwashing prevents the spread of many common infections.  简单的洗手就能防止许多常见感染传播。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facilitate</td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变容易，促进</td><td>facilitate the discussion<br>促进讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new airport will facilitate tourism throughout the whole region.  新机场将促进整个地区的旅游业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foreword</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrwɜːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>前言</td><td>write the foreword<br>撰写前言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A famous historian wrote the foreword to her first book.  一位著名历史学家为她的第一本书撰写了前言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beginning</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>最初的，开端的；开始</td><td>the beginning stages<br>起始阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We are still at the beginning of the investigation.  我们仍处于调查的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 introduction</td><td>/ˌɪntrəˈdʌkʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>引言，导论；介绍</td><td>a brief introduction<br>简短的引言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book opens with a brief introduction by the editor.  这本书以编者的简短引言开篇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overture</td><td>/ˈoʊvərtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序曲，前奏；提议</td><td>play the overture<br>演奏序曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra opened the evening with a lively overture by Rossini.  乐团以罗西尼一首轻快的序曲开始了晚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preamble</td><td>/ˈpriːæmbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>序言，开场白</td><td>the preamble to the treaty<br>条约的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The preamble to the treaty states its guiding principles.  条约的序言阐明了其指导原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preface</td><td>/ˈprefəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序言</td><td>write a preface<br>写序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author used the preface to thank everyone who helped her research.  作者在序言中感谢了所有帮助她做研究的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prelude</td><td>/ˈpreɪˌluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序曲；前奏，先兆</td><td>a prelude to war<br>战争的前奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border clashes proved to be a prelude to full-scale war.  边境冲突后来成了全面战争的前奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prologue</td><td>/ˈproʊlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序言</td><td>the prologue to the play<br>该剧的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prologue to the play explains how the bitter feud began.  该剧的序言说明了这场激烈仇怨的由来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 epilogue</td><td>/ˈepɪlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>文学作品的结局</td><td>a brief epilogue<br>简短的结语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief epilogue tells the reader what became of the two brothers.  简短的结语告诉读者两兄弟后来的境况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 coda</td><td>/ˈkoʊdə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终曲</td><td>a triumphant coda<br>辉煌的终曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The symphony ends with a quiet and unexpectedly gentle coda.  这部交响曲以安静而出乎意料温柔的尾声结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forge</b></td><td>/fɔːrdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>伪造；锻造，铸就</td><td>forge a passport<br>伪造护照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was arrested for forging a passport and two visas.  他因伪造一本护照和两个签证而被捕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coin</td><td>/ˈkɔɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创造（新词）；硬币</td><td>coin a phrase<br>创造一个说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists coined the term to describe the new particle.  科学家创造了这个术语来描述这种新粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counterfeit</td><td>/ˈkaʊntərfɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>仿制的，假冒的；仿制</td><td>counterfeit banknotes<br>伪造的钞票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police seized thousands of counterfeit banknotes hidden in the delivery van.  警方缴获了藏在送货车里的数千张假钞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabricate</td><td>/ˈfæbrɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>捏造</td><td>fabricate evidence<br>伪造证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had fabricated evidence to protect his own brother.  那名证人捏造证据来保护自己的兄弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fake</td><td>/ˈfeɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>假的，伪造的；伪造品</td><td>a fake diploma<br>假文凭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery unknowingly displayed a fake for almost ten years.  这家画廊不知情地展出了一件赝品近十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 build</td><td>/ˈbɪld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>建造，建立；体格</td><td>build a bridge<br>建造一座桥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They plan to build a new library on the empty lot downtown.  他们计划在市中心的空地上建一座新图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 construct</td><td>/kənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>建造，建立；创造，想出</td><td>construct a theory<br>构建一套理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer constructed a careful argument from a handful of ordinary documents.  律师用少量普通文件构建出一套严密的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 form</td><td>/ˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>构成，形成；形式</td><td>form a committee<br>组成一个委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Six neighbors formed a committee to repair the shared path.  六位邻居组成委员会修理共用的小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make</td><td>/ˈmeɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制造，做成；使成为</td><td>make a wooden chair<br>做一把木椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather could make a wooden chair with only simple tools.  我祖父只用简单工具就能做出一把木椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manufacture</td><td>/ˌmænjəˈfæktʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>制造；编造，虚构</td><td>manufacture an alibi<br>编造不在场证明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had manufactured an alibi that fell apart quickly.  证人编造的不在场证明很快就站不住脚了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mold</td><td>/ˈmoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>塑造，形成；模具</td><td>mold public opinion<br>塑造公众舆论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newspapers helped mold public opinion during the war.  战争期间报纸帮助塑造了公众舆论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shape</td><td>/ˈʃeɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使成形，塑造</td><td>shape public opinion<br>塑造公众舆论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early experiences shape the way children see the world.  早期经历塑造了孩子看世界的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>formidable</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrmɪdəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以对付的，令人敬畏的</td><td>a formidable opponent<br>难以对付的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a formidable opponent in the final round.  她在最后一轮遇到了难以对付的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 challenging</td><td>/ˈtʃæləndʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有挑战性的，费心力的</td><td>a challenging assignment<br>有挑战性的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor gave us a challenging assignment due next week.  教授给了我们一个下周交的有挑战性作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 demanding</td><td>/dɪˈmændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难取悦的，难满足的；费时间花心思的</td><td>a demanding new job<br>费心费力的新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for three small children is demanding work with no holidays.  照顾三个小孩是费时间花心思且没有休假的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæktɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，苛求的；费时间花心思的</td><td>exacting quality standards<br>严格的质量标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline maintains exacting standards for every part it installs.  这家航空公司对安装的每个部件都保持严格的标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grueling</td><td>/ˈɡruːəlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>累人的，折磨人的</td><td>a grueling schedule<br>折磨人的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team endured a grueling schedule of twelve games in three weeks.  球队熬过了三周十二场比赛这种折磨人的赛程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavy</td><td>/ˈhevi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重的，沉重的</td><td>too heavy to lift<br>重得抬不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The box was so heavy that two men could barely move it.  这箱子太重，两个人几乎搬不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laborious</td><td>/ləˈbɔːriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰苦的；勤奋的</td><td>a laborious process<br>费力的过程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying the manuscript by hand proved a slow and laborious process.  手抄这份手稿是一个缓慢而费力的过程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sweaty</td><td>/ˈsweti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，累人的；出汗的</td><td>sweaty work<br>累人的活儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moving the piano upstairs was hot, sweaty work.  把钢琴搬上楼是又热又累人的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 toilsome</del></td><td>/ˈtɔɪlsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辛苦的，费力的</td><td>toilsome labor<br>辛苦的劳作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Copying manuscripts by hand was toilsome work that ruined many eyes.  手抄书稿是毁坏许多人眼睛的辛苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tough</td><td>/ˈtʌf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>艰难的，费力的；坚韧的</td><td>a tough assignment<br>艰巨的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing that manuscript proved a tough assignment.  编辑那份手稿被证明是项艰巨的任务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strenuous</td><td>/ˈstrenjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>费力的，艰巨的；奋力的</td><td>strenuous exercise<br>费力的运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors advised him to avoid strenuous exercise for at least a month.  医生建议他至少一个月内避免费力的运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easy</td><td>/ˈiːzi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易的，不费力的</td><td>an easy question<br>简单的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The homework looked easy, but it took him three hours to finish.  作业看起来容易，可他花了三个小时才做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 effortless</td><td>/ˈefərtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松的，不费力的</td><td>effortless grace<br>毫不费力的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her swimming looked effortless even in the rough water.  即使在波涛中她的游泳也显得毫不费力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 facile</td><td>/ˈfæsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易做到的；肤浅轻率的</td><td>a facile explanation<br>肤浅的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report offers a facile explanation for a very complex problem.  这份报告对一个非常复杂的问题给出了肤浅的解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindless</td><td>/ˈmaɪndləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不需用脑的；愚蠢的</td><td>mindless routine work<br>不需用脑的例行工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted mindless work that let him rest his brain.  他想做点不用脑的活儿，让大脑休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>forthright</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrθraɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>直率的，说话率真的；直接的，不绕弯子的</td><td>a forthright answer<br>直率的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His forthright answer surprised the reporters who expected evasion.  他直率的回答让原本预期他闪避的记者很意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 candid</td><td>/ˈkændəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坦率的，直言的</td><td>a candid assessment<br>坦率的评价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a candid assessment of the team's weaknesses.  她对团队的弱点作了坦率的评价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forthcoming</td><td>/ˌfɔːrθˈkʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐于提供信息的；即将到来的</td><td>not very forthcoming<br>不太肯透露</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness was not very forthcoming about that night.  证人对那晚的事不太肯透露。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frank</td><td>/ˈfræŋk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坦白的，直率的</td><td>to be frank with you<br>跟你说实话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let me be frank with you about the risks involved.  让我坦白地告诉你其中的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outspoken</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈspoʊkən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直言不讳的</td><td>an outspoken critic<br>直言不讳的批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an outspoken critic of the new policy.  她是新政策直言不讳的批评者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 straightforward</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单明了的；坦率的</td><td>a straightforward procedure<br>简单直接的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions are straightforward enough for a child to follow.  说明简单明了，连孩子都能照着做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 direct</td><td>/dəˈrekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直接的，径直的</td><td>a direct flight<br>直飞航班</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ask for a direct answer instead of a long explanation.  要个直接的答复，而不是长篇解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 straight</td><td>/ˈstreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直的，笔直的</td><td>a straight road<br>笔直的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drive straight ahead until you reach the second traffic light.  一直往前开，直到第二个红绿灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissembling</td><td>/dɪˈsemblɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>掩饰真情，假装</td><td>tired of his dissembling<br>厌倦他的掩饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After months of dissembling he finally admitted the truth.  掩饰了数月之后，他终于承认了真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 circuitous</td><td>/sərˈkjuːɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，不直接的</td><td>a circuitous route<br>迂回的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took a circuitous route to avoid the flooded highway.  我们绕了一条迂回的路以避开被淹的公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indirect</td><td>/ˌɪndəˈrekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迂回的，间接的</td><td>an indirect route<br>迂回的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We took an indirect route to avoid the flooded highway.  我们走了绕行路线以避开被淹的公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 roundabout</td><td>/ˈraʊndəˌbaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>迂回的，间接的</td><td>in a roundabout way<br>以迂回的方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told me the news in a roundabout way that confused everyone.  她以绕弯子的方式告诉我这消息，把大家都搞糊涂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fortify</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harden</td><td>/ˈhɑːrdən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使变硬；使变冷酷坚强</td><td>harden into stone<br>变硬得像石头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of war had hardened the young soldier's heart.  多年战争使这名年轻士兵的心变得冷硬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invigorate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使精神，使强壮，鼓舞激励</td><td>invigorate the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk in cold air always invigorates my tired mind.  在冷空气中短暂散步总能让我疲惫的头脑重新振作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 toughen</td><td>/ˈtʌfən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使坚强，强化</td><td>toughen the rules<br>强化规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government plans to toughen the rules on emissions.  政府计划强化排放规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vitiate</td><td>/ˈvɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，损害；使无效</td><td>vitiate an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single false statement can vitiate the entire contract.  一句虚假陈述就能使整份合同失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weaken</td><td>/ˈwiːkən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，使变弱，变衰弱</td><td>weaken the structure<br>使结构变弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated flooding weakened the foundations of the stone bridge.  反复的洪水削弱了石桥的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>foster</b></td><td>/ˈfɔːstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，鼓励，培养</td><td>foster cooperation<br>促进合作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new program fosters cooperation between local schools and museums.  新项目促进当地学校与博物馆之间的合作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advance</td><td>/ədˈvæns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>前进；促进；预付款</td><td>advance a theory<br>提出一种理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The army advanced slowly through the thick forest at dawn.  黎明时军队缓慢穿过密林前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encourage</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，激励；促进</td><td>encourage students to read<br>鼓励学生阅读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers encourage students to ask difficult questions.  好老师鼓励学生提出有难度的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forward</td><td>/ˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>向前的；前部的</td><td>take a forward step<br>向前迈一步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leaned forward to hear the quiet speaker better.  他向前倾身以便听清声音轻的讲话者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 further</td><td>/ˈfɜːrðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>促进，推动；更远的</td><td>further his career<br>促进他的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took the job abroad to further her research on glaciers.  她接受海外工作以推进她的冰川研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incubate</td><td>/ˈɪŋkjubeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>孵化；帮助，培养，促进</td><td>incubate a startup<br>孵化一家初创公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university incubates dozens of small companies every single year.  这所大学每年孵化数十家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourage</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁；劝阻</td><td>discourage him from trying<br>劝他不要尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early failure should not discourage a young researcher.  早期的失败不该让年轻研究者气馁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inhibit</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪbɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，阻碍</td><td>inhibit the growth of bacteria<br>抑制细菌生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold weather inhibits the growth of these tropical plants.  寒冷天气抑制了这些热带植物的生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>founder</b></td><td>/ˈfaʊndər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>创立者；彻底失败，沉没</td><td>the founder of the school<br>这所学校的创办者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The peace talks foundered on the difficult question of wages.  和谈在棘手的工资问题上完全失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 architect</td><td>/ˈɑːrkəˌtekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>缔造者，设计者；建筑师</td><td>the architect of the reform<br>这场改革的缔造者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is widely seen as the architect of the reform.  他被普遍视为这场改革的缔造者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 author</td><td>/ˈɔːθər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>创始者，制订者；作者</td><td>the author of the plan<br>该计划的制订者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator was the author of the original bill.  这位参议员是原始法案的制订者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creator</td><td>/kriˈeɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>创造者，创始人</td><td>the creator of the series<br>该剧集的创作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The creator of the series also wrote its theme song.  该剧集的创作者还写了它的主题曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 father</td><td>/ˈfɑːðər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>父亲；创始人，先驱</td><td>the father of modern physics<br>现代物理学之父</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Galileo is often called the father of modern science.  伽利略常被称为现代科学之父。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generator</td><td>/ˈdʒenəreɪtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>产生者，创始者；发电机</td><td>a generator of new ideas<br>新想法的源头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab became a generator of remarkably original ideas.  该实验室成了极具原创性想法的源头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 initiator</td><td>/ɪˈnɪʃieɪtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>创始者，发起人</td><td>the initiator of the project<br>该项目的发起人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was the initiator of the citywide recycling program.  她是全市回收计划的发起人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 originator</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒəneɪtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>创始人，首创者</td><td>the originator of the theory<br>该理论的首创者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few remember the originator of this famous theory.  很少有人记得这一著名理论的首创者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sire</td><td>/ˈsaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>生父，祖先；生育</td><td>sire many offspring<br>生育众多后代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That champion stallion sired more than forty foals.  那匹冠军种马生育了四十多匹小马。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collapse</td><td>/kəˈlæps/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>失败</td><td>the collapse of talks<br>谈判的破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The collapse of the talks surprised nobody in the capital.  谈判的破裂在首都没让任何人感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flunk</td><td>/ˈflʌŋk/</td><td>★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>失败，不及格</td><td>flunk the exam<br>考试不及格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He flunked the exam and had to repeat the course.  他考试不及格，只好重修这门课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miss</td><td>/ˈmɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>未成功，错失</td><td>miss the target<br>未命中目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company missed its sales target for a third year.  公司连续第三年未达成销售目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strike out</td><td>/straɪk ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>彻底失败；三振出局</td><td>strike out completely<br>彻底失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He struck out completely in his first attempt at business.  他第一次创业彻底失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wash out</td><td>/wɑʃ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>失败，被淘汰；冲掉</td><td>wash out of the program<br>被项目淘汰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the trainees washed out of the program by June.  到六月一半受训者被项目淘汰了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terminator</td><td>/ˈtɜːrməneɪtər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>终结者，终止者</td><td>the terminator of the dynasty<br>王朝的终结者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as the terminator of a long dynasty.  历史把他记作一个漫长王朝的终结者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succeed</td><td>/səkˈsiːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>成功；接替</td><td>succeed in business<br>在生意上成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She succeeded in business where her brothers had failed.  她在兄弟们失败的生意上取得了成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 work out</td><td>/wɜːrk ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>成功，取得好结果；锻炼</td><td>things worked out well<br>事情结果不错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the end, everything worked out better than expected.  最后一切结果都比预期更好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fracas</b></td><td>/ˈfreɪkəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>争吵，吵闹</td><td>a fracas in the bar<br>酒吧里的一场吵闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A noisy fracas broke out in the bar after midnight.  午夜过后酒吧里爆发了一场吵闹的争斗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 battle</td><td>/ˈbætəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>斗争，交战；战斗</td><td>a legal battle<br>一场法律争斗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms are locked in a long legal battle.  两家公司陷入一场漫长的法律争斗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clash</td><td>/ˈklæʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>冲突，争执</td><td>a clash with police<br>与警方的冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Protesters clashed with police outside the courthouse.  抗议者在法院外与警方发生冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combat</td><td>/ˈkɑːmbæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>战斗，格斗；对抗</td><td>hand-to-hand combat<br>近身格斗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers were trained in hand-to-hand combat.  这些士兵受过近身格斗训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contest</td><td>/ˈkɑːntest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>争夺，争斗；比赛</td><td>a bitter contest<br>激烈的争夺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The election became a bitter contest between old friends.  这场选举变成了旧友之间的激烈争夺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hassle</td><td>/ˈhæsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>争吵；麻烦事</td><td>a hassle over money<br>为钱的一场争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had a long hassle over who should pay.  他们为该谁付钱争吵了很久。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skirmish</td><td>/ˈskɜːrmɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小规模冲突，小争执</td><td>a border skirmish<br>边境小冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief skirmish broke out along the disputed border.  争议边境沿线爆发了一场短暂冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 struggle</td><td>/ˈstrʌɡəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>挣扎，费力地做；斗争</td><td>struggle to keep up<br>挣扎着跟上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He struggled to keep up with the rest of the class.  他挣扎着跟上班上其他同学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tussle</td><td>/ˈtʌsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>争斗，扭打</td><td>tussle over the ball<br>为争球而扭打</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two players tussled over the ball near the goal.  两名球员在球门附近为争球扭打起来。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>fracture</b></td><td>/ˈfræktʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>破裂</td><td>a hairline fracture<br>一道细微裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan revealed a hairline fracture in his left wrist.  扫描显示他左手腕有一道细微裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rupture</td><td>/ˈrʌptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>打破；打碎，破裂</td><td>rupture a blood vessel<br>使血管破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old pipe could rupture at any moment under such high pressure.  这根旧管道随时可能在如此高的压力下破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 schism</td><td>/ˈskɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分裂，派系对立</td><td>a bitter schism<br>严重的分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bitter schism split the party into two hostile factions.  一场激烈的分裂把该党撕成两个敌对派系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrupt</td><td>/dɪsˈrʌpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扰乱，打乱</td><td>disrupt the meeting<br>扰乱会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Protesters disrupted the ceremony by shouting from the balcony.  抗议者在楼上喊叫，扰乱了典礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fragment</td><td>/ˈfræɡmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>碎片；使破碎，分裂</td><td>fragment into pieces<br>碎裂成片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old empire fragmented into a dozen small states.  旧帝国分裂成了十几个小国。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 rive</td><td>/raɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>撕开</td><td>rive the log apart<br>把木头劈开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lightning can rive a tall oak straight down the middle of its trunk.  闪电能把高大的橡树从树干正中劈开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unity</td><td>/ˈjuːnəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>统一，团结；整体性</td><td>national unity<br>国家统一</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech was an appeal for national unity.  这篇演讲是对国家团结的呼吁。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breakable</td><td>/ˈbreɪkəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的，可打破的</td><td>breakable items<br>易碎物品</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frail</td><td>/freɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；脆弱的</td><td>a frail old woman<br>体弱的老妇人</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friable</td><td>/ˈfraɪəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的</td><td>friable dry soil<br>易碎的干土</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frangible</td><td>/ˈfrændʒəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的，脆弱的</td><td>frangible material<br>易碎的材料</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infirm</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；不果断的，优柔寡断的</td><td>old and infirm<br>年老体弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother grew too infirm to climb the stairs without help.  她的祖母身体太虚弱，没人帮忙就爬不了楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsound</td><td>/ənˈsaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，有缺陷的</td><td>unsound reasoning<br>有缺陷的推理</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effete</td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enervated</td><td>/ˈɛnərveɪtəd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，无力的</td><td>enervated by the heat<br>被暑热弄得虚弱</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 feeble</td><td>/ˈfiːbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的</td><td>a feeble voice<br>微弱的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man answered in a feeble voice from the bed.  老人从床上用微弱的声音回答。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 fragile</td><td>/ˈfrædʒl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的</td><td>a fragile vase<br>易碎的花瓶</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infirm</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的；不果断的，优柔寡断的</td><td>old and infirm<br>年老体弱</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsubstantial</td><td>/ˌʌnsəbˈstænʃəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不结实的；空洞无实质的</td><td>unsubstantial evidence<br>缺乏实质的证据</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 characterless</td><td>/ˈkɛrəktərləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无特色的；缺乏骨气的</td><td>a characterless building<br>毫无特色的建筑</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invertebrate</td><td>/ɪnˈvɜːrtəbrət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>没有骨气的，软弱的；无脊椎动物</td><td>an invertebrate politician<br>没有骨气的政客</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nerveless</td><td>/ˈnɜːrvləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>软弱无力的；镇定的</td><td>nerveless fingers<br>软弱无力的手指</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spineless</td><td>/ˈspaɪnləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没骨气的，软弱的</td><td>a spineless coward<br>没骨气的胆小鬼</td></tr>
